# followup-combat-batch-f: several captures of the sweep in one run (one emulator lock); each part starts with a thaw and a restore. Parts: the ejection first (the height poked higher in the dive snapshot so that the seat spawns before the aircraft hits the sea), then the crash with the ejection nibble at 6 (note 07 6.4, open question A8); a hostile long-range missile (class 1) 45 passes behind the player: the threat byte V:4A925
# --- part crashafter2
thaw
restore extracted/captures/dive-precrash.vasnap
seglist extracted/interceptor.bin extracted/interceptor.map.json

keydown LSHIFT
key E 0.12
keyup LSHIFT
